# The Landfill Tax Regulations 1996

> Text as at 2025-11-01.
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

1996 No. 1527

LANDFILL TAX

The Landfill Tax Regulations 1996 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

Made *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

12th June 1996 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

Laid before the House of Commons *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

12th June 1996 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

Coming into force *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

1st August 1996 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

The Commissioners of Customs and Excise, in exercise of the powers conferred on them by sections 47(9), 48(1) and (2), 49, 51(1) to (6), 52(1) to (3), 53(1) to (4), 58(1) and (4) to (6), 61(2), 62(1) to (3) and (5) and (6) and 68(1) to (6) of, and paragraphs 2(1) to (3), 13(1) and (6), 14(5), 20(3), 23(1), 42(1) to (5) and 43(1) to (5) of Schedule 5 to, the Finance Act 1996 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443][^f00001], hereby make the following Regulations: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3a631ceec175ea3fe9bd0b6045e94443]

## PART I (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — PRELIMINARY (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Citation and commencement (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-4944308b796bbadf3f7fbb0dfe93ce12][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

These Regulations may be cited as the Landfill Tax Regulations 1996 and shall come into force on 1st August 1996. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-23f985b1af7ccfb413263049d9bc8aad][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In these Regulations— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- [^key-caf90316e07ed48b82fe4734e0886245]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “accounting period” has the meaning given in regulation 10;[^key-faadbe4ef4d7ed3fffbf00ec9dab85ce] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “the Act” means the Finance Act 1996; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “Collector” means a Collector, Deputy Collector or Assistant Collector of Customs and Excise; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “credit”, except where the context otherwise requires, means credit which a person is entitled to claim under Part IV of these Regulations; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “disposal” and “disposed of” shall be construed in accordance with section 40A of the Act (including any order made under that section or section 46 of the Act) (save in regulation 21(4)(c));[^key-3c01ddbee1b80e883e50a0200ecc7347] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “environmental regulator” means the Environment Agency in England and the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs, or any agency thereof, in Northern Ireland;[^key-7bdfba3119dc070332bb2f72f3d593f1] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “effective date of registration” means the date determined in accordance with section 47 of the Act upon which the person was or should have been registered; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “landfill invoice” means an invoice of the description in regulation 37; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “landfill site” has the meaning given in *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] section 40(4)[^key-36a8f047c63033472cc20c8fe5602d15] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] of the Act; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “landfill tax account” has the meaning given in regulation 12; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “landfill tax bad debt account” has the meaning given in regulation 26; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “LOI percentage” means the amount of non-qualifying material contained in fines, as indicated by the percentage of the mass of those fines lost on ignition;[^key-1862a4819c93109633cff010c39e7c47] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “LOI test” means a test to determine the LOI percentage of fines, conducted in accordance with the terms of a published notice made under the Landfill Tax (Qualifying Fines) Order 2015 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or the Landfill Tax (Qualifying Fines) (No. 2) Order 2015[^key-158d93a83faf7d6ca60f00e995c1f949] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “quarter” means a period of three months ending at the end of March, June, September or December; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “registered person” means a person who is registered under section 47 of the Act and “register” and “registration” shall be construed accordingly; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “registrable person” has the meaning given in section 47(10) of the Act; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “registration number” means the identifying number allocated to a registered person and notified to him by the Commissioners; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “return” means a return which is required to be made in accordance with regulation 11; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “taxable business” means a business or part of a business in the course of which taxable activities are carried out; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “transfer note” means written information or a transfer note within the meaning of—[^key-3818ed4a578227d5551e234cdd0318a3] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) the Waste (England and Wales) Regulations 2011, where the transferor of material to a landfill site is a person in England and Wales; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) the Controlled Waste (Duty of Care) Regulations (Northern Ireland) 2002,where the transferor of material to a landfill site is a person in Northern Ireland; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (c) the Environmental Protection (Duty of Care) (Scotland) Regulations 2014, where the transferor of material to a landfill site is a person in Scotland. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “working day” means any day of the week except Saturday and Sunday and a bank holiday or public holiday, in either case, for England. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In these Regulations any question whether a person is connected with another shall be determined in accordance with *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] section 1122 of the Corporation Tax Act 2010[^key-a817106f71bcc9e33e44b88eaf21ae4e] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any reference in these Regulations to “this Part” is a reference to the Part of these Regulations in which that reference is made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any reference in these Regulations to a form prescribed in the Schedule to these Regulations shall include a reference to a form which the Commissioners are satisfied is a form to the like effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Designation, direction or approval (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31bfbb200753b7e633393c4c9666122b][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

Any designation, direction or approval by the Commissioners under or for the purposes of these Regulations shall be made or given by a notice in writing. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART II (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — REGISTRATION AND PROVISION FOR SPECIAL CASES (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Notification of liability to be registered (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-bfbf8b149c41216ed8bce66a76685fd6][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person who is required by section 47(3) of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to notify the Commissioners of his intention to carry out taxable activities shall do so on the form numbered 1 in the Schedule to these Regulations. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-ae90a6e805e9e573dcf5ad2f2dcdc536][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A registered person who is required by section 47(3A) of the Act to notify the Commissioners of their intention to carry out taxable activities shall do so by providing the information prescribed by paragraph (6). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a notification[^key-81aef21210859a18c5c256ddeb7d5e5d] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] referred to in this regulation is made by a person who operates or intends to operate more than one landfill site *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or place other than a landfill site[^key-de45364dcb57e709be7b1ac2208aafa2] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], it shall include the particulars set out on the form numbered 2 in the Schedule to these Regulations. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a notification[^key-7f0ea103d966d9a86ae5b663e20b9d0b] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] referred to in this regulation is made by a partnership, it shall include the particulars set out on the form numbered 3 in the Schedule to these Regulations. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (5), a[^key-04fb2f7cbf298ba8ef56a3275b0448c9] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] notification referred to in this regulation shall be made within 30 days of the earliest date *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-28d12519c1259a3950489f2d4517f19a]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] on which the person either forms or continues to have the intention to carry out taxable activities. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5)[^key-06a9fcc2b1740ea486795e54a22c0018] The Commissioners may allow a registered person additional time to comply with paragraph (4) in relation to a notification made under section 47(3A) of the Act.

(6) For the purposes of paragraph (1A), the prescribed information required from a registered person is—

- (a) their name and address and, where applicable, company registration number;
- (b) their contact telephone number;
- (c) if a business, whether they are a sole trader, partnership or a company;
- (d) the address of each place other than a landfill site at which they intend to carry out taxable activities;
- (e) the details of any licence or permit issued by an environmental regulator in respect of each place;
- (f) whether any of the intended disposals are within the scope of any published guidance issued by an environmental regulator in the form of a regulatory position statement;
- (g) if any disposals have already taken place at any place other than a landfill site at the time of notification, the address of each place at which such disposal occurred and the volume of the material disposed of;
- (h) a signed declaration that the information provided under the preceding sub-paragraphs is true and complete.

### 5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Changes in particulars (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-562c79590d29ee0697e2a72a8efb66ee][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person who has made a notification under regulation 4, whether or not it was made in accordance with paragraph (4) of that regulation, shall, within 30 days of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] discovering any inaccuracy in; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any change occurring which causes to become inaccurate, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

any of the information which was contained in or provided with the notification, notify the Commissioners in writing and furnish them with full particulars. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Without prejudice to paragraph (1) above, a registrable person shall, within 30 days of any change occurring in any of the circumstances referred to in paragraph (4) below, notify the Commissioners in writing and furnish them with particulars of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the change; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date on which the change occurred. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A registrable person who discovers that any information contained in or provided with a notification under paragraph (1) or (2) above was inaccurate shall, within 30 days of his discovering the inaccuracy, notify the Commissioners in writing and furnish them with particulars of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the inaccuracy; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date on which the inaccuracy was discovered; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] how the information was inaccurate; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the correct information. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The circumstances mentioned in paragraph (2) above are the following circumstances relating to the registrable person or any taxable business carried on by him: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] his name, his trading name (if different), his address and the landfill sites he operates; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] his status, namely whether he carries on business as a sole proprietor, body corporate, partnership or other unincorporated body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of a partnership, the name and address of any partner. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any person failing to comply with a requirement imposed in any of paragraphs (1) to (3) above shall be liable to a penalty of £250. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where in relation to a registered person the Commissioners are satisfied that any of the information recorded in the register is or has become inaccurate they may correct the register accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraph (6) above, it is immaterial whether or not the registered person has notified the Commissioners of any change which has occurred in accordance with paragraphs (1) to (3) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Notification of cessation of taxable activities (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-1102e4b8a3451ed40ed3d34bad30843d][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

A person who is required by section 47(4) of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to notify the Commissioners of his having ceased to have the intention to carry out taxable activities shall, within 30 days of his so having ceased, notify the Commissioners in writing and shall therein inform them of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date on which he ceased to have the intention of carrying out taxable activities; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if different, the date on which he ceased to carry out taxable activities. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 6A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Registration: taxable activities at places other than a landfill site (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-0468d4d2da81c09a589053fecc881a8d][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] This regulation applies to taxable disposals of material— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] at a place other than a landfill site; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] where it is not known whether the place of disposal is a landfill site at the time that any matter falls for consideration by the Commissioners under this regulation. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (3), the Commissioners may not register a person (“P”) who carries out taxable activities at a place other than a landfill site. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where it is not known whether a place is a landfill site, the Commissioners may register P on a provisional basis, subject to the conditions prescribed in paragraph (4). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The conditions referred to in paragraph (3) are that— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Commissioners may require security under paragraph 31 of Schedule 5 to the Act from P during the period of provisional registration as if the site were a place other than a landfill site; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Commissioners may require P to preserve records in accordance with regulations 16 and 16ZZA; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any period of provisional registration shall end— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when it is agreed or finally determined that a place is a place other than a landfill site; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if the Commissioners are provided by P with any information that is or becomes inaccurate; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if P fails to account for any tax, make any returns, pay any tax, provide any security required by the Commissioners, or preserve any records required by them to be preserved; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in circumstances where a period of provisional registration ends under sub-paragraph (c), the Commissioners may determine that P shall be treated as if P had never been registered; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if it is agreed or finally determined that a place is a landfill site, P must be registered with effect from the time that P began taxable activities. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In paragraph (4)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] “agreed” means agreed between P and the Commissioners; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] “finally determined” means that— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] an environmental regulator has determined whether a place is a place other than a landfill site and any dispute as to that determination has been resolved by agreement or otherwise finally concluded; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] an appeal under section 55(1)(a) of the Act brought by P against a decision by the Commissioners to decline to register them on the ground contained in paragraph (3) has been finally determined. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Transfer of a going concern (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3cc97d889fb5045191638e575459b12c][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a taxable business is transferred as a going concern; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (ab (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-61c313117f01f0c5a23b7a9aef79cc8e][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that business includes taxable activities that are carried out by a registered person; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the registration of the transferor has not already been cancelled; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] as a result of the transfer of the business the registration of the transferor is to be cancelled and the transferee has become liable to be registered; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] an application is made on the form numbered 4 in the Schedule to these Regulations by both the transferor and the transferee, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

the Commissioners may with effect from the date of the transfer cancel the registration of the transferor and register the transferee with the registration number previously allocated to the transferor. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] An application under paragraph (1) above shall be treated as the notification referred to in regulation 6. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the transferee of a business has been registered under paragraph (1) above with the registration number previously allocated to the transferor— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any liability of the transferor existing at the date of the transfer to make a return or account for or pay any tax under Part III of these Regulations shall become the liability of the transferee; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any entitlement of the transferor, whether or not existing at the date of the transfer, to credit or payment under Part IV of these Regulations shall become the entitlement of the transferee. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In addition to the provisions set out in paragraph (3) above, where the transferee of a business has been registered under paragraph (1) above with the registration number previously allocated to the transferor during an accounting period subsequent to that in which the transfer took place (but with effect from the date of the transfer) and any— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] return has been made; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] tax has been accounted for; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] entitlement to credit has been claimed, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

by either the transferor or the transferee, it shall be treated as having been done by the transferee. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a taxable business is transferred as a going concern; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (ab (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-09dab9c588da6b83807a4b42c728530f][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that business includes taxable activities that are carried out by a registered person; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the transferee removes material as described in regulation 21(2) or (4); and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the transferor has paid tax on the disposal concerned, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

then, whether or not the transferee has been registered under paragraph (1) above with the registration number previously allocated to the transferor, any entitlement to credit arising under Part V of these Regulations shall become the entitlement of the transferee. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Representation of unincorporated body (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-938d4b186206bcebe8630d2b1faeffe8][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where anything is required to be done by or under *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] (whether by these Regulations or otherwise) by or on behalf of an unincorporated body other than a partnership, it shall be the joint and several responsibility of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] every member holding office as president, chairman, treasurer, secretary or any similar office; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if there is no such office, every member holding office as a member of a committee by which the affairs of the body are managed; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if there is no such office or committee, every member; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but, subject to paragraph (2) below, if it is done by any of the persons referred to above that shall be sufficient compliance with any such requirement. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where an unincorporated body other than a partnership is required to make any notification such as is referred to in regulations 4 to 6, it shall not be sufficient compliance unless the notification is made by a person upon whom a responsibility for making it is imposed by paragraph (1) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where anything is required to be done by or under *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] (whether by these Regulations or otherwise) by or on behalf of a partnership, it shall be the joint and several responsibility of every partner; but if it is done by one partner or, in the case of a partnership whose principal place of business is in Scotland, by any other person authorised by the partnership with respect thereto that shall be sufficient compliance with any such requirement. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Bankruptcy or incapacity of registrable persons (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-747a9d7b4b7a67a26f0bd21dd154e881][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] If a registrable person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] carrying out disposals at a landfill site[^key-fa6833b3bfc4dbc14f7c3360c0ea35d0] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] becomes bankrupt or incapacitated, the Commissioners may, from the date on which he became bankrupt or incapacitated, as the case may be, treat as a registrable person any person carrying on any taxable business of his; and any legislation relating to landfill tax shall apply to any person so treated as though he were a registered person. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any person carrying on such business as aforesaid shall, within 30 days of commencing to do so, inform the Commissioners in writing of that fact and the date of the bankruptcy order or of the nature of the incapacity and the date on which it began. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the Commissioners have treated a person carrying on a business as a registrable person under paragraph (1) above, they shall cease so to treat him if— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the registration of the registrable person is cancelled, whether or not any other person is registered with the registration number previously allocated to him; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the bankruptcy is discharged or the incapacity ceases; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he ceases carrying on the business of the registrable person. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In relation to a registrable person which is a company, the references in this regulation to the registrable person becoming incapacitated shall be construed as references to its going into liquidation or receivership or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] entering administration[^key-37eb436745dcf91220b4ad9b42cf10b8] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; and references to the incapacity ceasing shall be construed accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART III (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — ACCOUNTING, PAYMENT (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) , RECORDS AND INFORMATION AREAS (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-6e0eb31f147d7b5169fb4ecc6f15dea4][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 10 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-f0eb02542dbfd64522fcc825b2395006][^key-5e1b33fa34dd73c1cce8b08770d7f691][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In this Part, an accounting period of a registered person means each period of three months ending on the dates notified to that person by the Commissioners, whether by means of a registration certificate issued by them or otherwise. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In every case, the first accounting period of a registered person shall begin on the effective date of registration. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 11 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Making of returns (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-6608d859640fa188a18d59c9c2522375][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (3) below and save as the Commissioners may otherwise allow, a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-1fcbb22ac1bf00e777920ec665d9a3ad] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall, in respect of each accounting period, make a return to the Controller, Central Collection Unit ( *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]LT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]), *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in a form that is determined by the Commissioners in a public notice.[^key-9f0a48f67ece6048a02d2e951ff703af] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (3) below, a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-5a171f5d0c93a0eefbad8ee94fa0b461] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall make each return not later than the last working day of the month next following the end of the period to which it relates. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the Commissioners consider it necessary in the circumstances of any particular case, they may— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] vary the length of any accounting period or the date on which it begins or ends or by which any return must be made; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] allow or direct the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-b8ac2b5a59857290794717733acd0d91] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to make a return in accordance with sub-paragraph (a) above; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] allow or direct a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to make returns to a specified address, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

and any person to whom the Commissioners give any direction such as is referred to in this regulation shall comply therewith. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 12 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Landfill tax account (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-9e6e162b4b141cba4ccae44f759bc37e][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Every *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-eef0c3b685edb48c4e8f8fbc8f4d6b1d] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall make and maintain an account to be known as “the landfill tax account”. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The landfill tax account shall be in such form and contain such particulars as may be stipulated in a notice published by the Commissioners and not withdrawn by a further notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 13 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Correction of errors (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-397f730f6c196608919fc552e078e374][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In this regulation— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- “overdeclaration” means, in relation to any return, the amount (if any) which was wrongly treated as tax due for the accounting period concerned and which caused the amount of tax which was payable to be overstated, or the entitlement to a payment under regulation 20 to be understated (or both) or would have caused such an overstatement or understatement were it not for the existence of an underdeclaration in relation to that return; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “underdeclaration” means, in relation to any return, the aggregate of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) the amount (if any) of tax due for the accounting period concerned which was not taken into account; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) the amount (if any) which was wrongly deducted as credit, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  and which caused the amount of tax which was payable to be understated, or the entitlement to a payment under regulation 20 to be overstated (or both) or would have caused such an understatement or overstatement were it not for the existence of an overdeclaration in relation to that return. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] This regulation applies where a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-751ef52a70ed4eb51637b9213feacbf5] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] has made a return which was inaccurate as the result of an overdeclaration or underdeclaration. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_6da7797b-e246-4dd9-cc91-327d12f09084][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where in any accounting period— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-f829fc85bbfc9a6f898eaef71b499e63] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] discovers one or more *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] overdeclarations or[^key-aa4b78a786a179f7cde8c03e91f1fc3c] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] underdeclarations; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] having treated the amount of those *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] overdeclarations or[^key-86ee815614c8515f685d8ad2d3a8b2b4] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] underdeclarations as reduced by the amount *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] respectively of any underdeclarations or overdeclarations[^key-c0e940b6cb611e3ecdeef8707069501d] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] for the same accounting periods, the total of those *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] overdeclarations or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] underdeclarations does not exceed *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] £50,000[^key-9476a73a7ef472fd784423e1b80b7dfc] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

he may enter the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] overdeclarations or[^key-f780e3efb69a4b14ff28acef42b66d99] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] underdeclarations in his return for the accounting period in which they were discovered by including their amount in *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the boxes for underdeclarations and overdeclarations as appropriate in the form referred to in regulation 11(1)[^key-3479ada2468159d1b8b9bf53dbeff413] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

But if the[^key-f2115d8fce798dd0ae7704200063a3d3] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]’s *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VAT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] turnover is small, the total mentioned in sub-paragraph (b) must not for these purposes exceed 1% of that turnover unless the total is *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] £10,000 or less[^key-d39fbc147045dfe16096aa9ea888f1cf] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

And if that person is not registered for *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VAT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], the total mentioned in sub-paragraph (b) must not for these purposes exceed £10,000. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

The[^key-13854d5fd648e474672f41fb87df6cc3] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VAT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] turnover is small only if Box 6 of that person’s value added tax return for the prescribed accounting period in which the discovery is made must contain a total less than £5,000,000 (total value of sales and all other outputs excluding any value added tax). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(See sections 96(1) and 1 of the Value Added Tax Act 1994 for “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]VAT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]” and “value added tax”, and sections 96(2) and 3 of and Schedules 1 to 3A to that Act about being registered for *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VAT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; regulations 2(1) and 25 of and Schedule 1 Forms 4 and 5 to the Value Added Tax Regulations 1995 for the meaning carried by “Box 6” and “return”; sections 96(1) and 25(1) of that Act and regulations 2(1), 25 and 99(1) of those Regulations for the meaning carried by “prescribed accounting period”; section 24(2) of that Act for “output tax”.) *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-8a3d16457004bc4ecf0667a989530a77] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] enters an amount in a return in accordance with paragraph *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-ea16a311a55e9d9cec3bc515453399c7]... (4) above he shall calculate the tax payable by him or the payment to which he is entitled accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where an amount has been entered in accordance with this regulation in a return which has been made— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the return shall be regarded as correcting any earlier return to which that amount relates; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] registered person[^key-77a97451d037089e9c342248cac95912] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall be taken to have furnished information with respect to the inaccuracy in the prescribed form and manner for the purposes of paragraph 20 of Schedule 5 to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] No amount shall be entered in a return in respect of any overdeclaration or underdeclaration except in accordance with this regulation; and as regards any underdeclaration that cannot be corrected under paragraph (4) above a person shall not be taken to have furnished information with respect to an inaccuracy in the prescribed form and manner for the purposes of paragraph 20 of Schedule 5 to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] unless he provides such information to the Commissioners in writing. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Claims for overpaid tax (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-c65002b4876f59abc4ddb4261409f5f0][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

Except where the amount to which the claim relates has been entered in a return in accordance with regulation 13 or is included in an amount so entered, any claim under paragraph 14 of Schedule 5 to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall be made in writing to the Commissioners and shall, by reference to such documentary evidence as is in the possession of the claimant, state the amount of the claim and the method by which that amount was calculated. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation of regulations 14A to 14H (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-c16cff2da7855913b144f79d67593789][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

In this regulation and in regulations 14B to 14H below— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- “claim” means a claim made (irrespective of when it was made) under paragraph 14 of Schedule 5 to the Act for repayment of an amount paid to the Commissioners by way of tax which was not tax due to them; and “claimed” and “claimant” shall be construed accordingly; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “reimbursement arrangements” means any arrangements (whether made before, on or after 30th January 1998) for the purposes of a claim which— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) are made by a claimant for the purpose of securing that he is not unjustly enriched by the repayment of any amount in pursuance of the claim; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) provide for the reimbursement of persons (consumers) who have, for practical purposes, borne the whole or any part of the cost of the original payment of that amount to the Commissioners; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “relevant amount” means that part (which may be the whole) of the amount of a claim which the claimant has reimbursed or intends to reimburse to consumers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Reimbursement arrangements—general (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

Without prejudice to regulation 14H below, for the purposes of paragraph 14(3) of Schedule 5 to the Act (defence by the Commissioners that repayment by them of an amount claimed would unjustly enrich the claimant) reimbursement arrangements made by a claimant shall be disregarded except where they— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] include the provisions described in regulation 14C below; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] are supported by the undertakings described in regulation 14G below. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Reimbursement arrangements—provisions to be included (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

The provisions referred to in regulation 14B(a) above are that— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] reimbursement for which the arrangements provide will be completed by no later than 90 days after the repayment to which it relates; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] no deduction will be made from the relevant amount by way of fee or charge (howsoever expressed or effected); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] reimbursement will be made only in cash or by cheque; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any part of the relevant amount that is not reimbursed by the time mentioned in paragraph (a) above will be repaid by the claimant to the Commissioners; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any interest paid by the Commissioners on any relevant amount repaid by them will also be treated by the claimant in the same way as the relevant amount falls to be treated under paragraphs (a) and (b) above; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the records described in regulation 14E below will be kept by the claimant and produced by him to the Commissioners, or to an officer of theirs in accordance with regulation 14F below. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14D (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Repayments to the Commissioners (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

The claimant shall, without prior demand, make any repayment to the Commissioners that he is required to make by virtue of regulation 14C(d) and (e) above within 14 days of the expiration of the period of 90 days referred to in regulation 14C(a) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14E (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Records (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

The claimant shall keep records of the following matters— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the names and addresses of those consumers whom he has reimbursed or whom he intends to reimburse; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the total amount reimbursed to each such consumer; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of interest included in each total amount reimbursed to each consumer; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date that each reimbursement is made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14F (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Production of records (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where a claimant is given notice in accordance with paragraph (2) below, he shall, in accordance with such notice produce to the Commissioners, or to an officer of theirs, the records that he is required to keep pursuant to regulation 14E above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A notice given for the purposes of paragraph (1) above shall— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] be in writing; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] state the place and time at which, and the date on which the records are to be produced; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] be signed and dated by the Commissioners, or by an officer of theirs, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

and may be given before or after, or both before and after the Commissioners have paid the relevant amount to the claimant. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14G (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Undertakings (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Without prejudice to regulation 14H(b) below, the undertakings referred to in regulation 14B(b) above shall be given to the Commissioners by the claimant no later than the time at which he makes the claim for which the reimbursement arrangements have been made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The undertakings shall be in writing, shall be signed and dated by the claimant, and shall be to the effect that— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] at the date of the undertakings he is able to identify the names and addresses of those consumers whom he has reimbursed or whom he intends to reimburse; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he will apply the whole of the relevant amount repaid to him, without any deduction by way of fee or charge or otherwise, to the reimbursement in cash or by cheque, of such consumers by no later than 90 days after his receipt of that amount (except insofar as he has already so reimbursed them); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he will apply any interest paid to him on the relevant amount repaid to him wholly to the reimbursement of such consumers by no later than 90 days after his receipt of that interest; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he will repay to the Commissioners without demand the whole or such part of the relevant amount repaid to him or of any interest paid to him as he fails to apply in accordance with the undertakings mentioned in sub-paragraphs (b) and (c) above; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he will keep the records described in regulation 14E above; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he will comply with any notice given to him in accordance with regulation 14F above concerning the production of such records. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 14H (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Reimbursement arrangements made before 11th February 1998 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

Reimbursement arrangements made by a claimant before 11th February 1998 shall not be disregarded for the purposes of paragraph 14(3) of Schedule 5 to the Act if, not later than 11th March 1998— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he includes in those arrangements (if they are not already included) the provisions described in regulation 14C above; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] gives the undertakings described in regulation 14G above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 15 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Payment of tax (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-e16cd6bc0f696d269345ee0708e59174][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

Save as the Commissioners may otherwise allow or direct, any person required to make a return shall pay to the Controller, Central Collection Unit ( *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]LT *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]), such amount of tax as is payable by him in respect of the accounting period to which the return relates no later than the last day on which he was required to make the return. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 16 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Records (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) : landfill sites (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-feaa68c427f5e1fdd7c9642506fabc97][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-a20939e3d989725a242d9a0a59db78ea][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Every registrable person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] carrying out taxable activities at a landfill site[^key-a843d20078309098877c9c196af244eb] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall, for the purpose of accounting for tax, preserve the following— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] his business and accounting records; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] his landfill tax account; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] transfer notes and any other original or copy records in relation to material brought onto or removed from the landfill site (including any record made *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] on or before 31st August 2009[^key-e6052f668a04b5447d0ca978a8010bba] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] for the purpose of Part IX of these Regulations *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] before that Part was revoked with effect from 1st September 2009, or for the purpose of regulation 16A(4) below[^key-7d98b2784a9bef3c203d28a128673236] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] all invoices (including landfill invoices) and similar documents issued to him and copies of such invoices and similar documents issued by him; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] all credit or debit notes or other documents received by him which evidence an increase or decrease in the amount of any consideration for a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] disposal[^key-e99cd953be0f01512c61251cc4c510e2] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], and copies of such documents that are issued by him; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] such other records as the Commissioners may specify in a notice published by them and not withdrawn by a further notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraphs (3) and (4) below, every registrable person shall preserve the records specified in paragraph (1) above for a period of six years. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (4) below, a registrable person who has made a landfill tax bad debt account shall preserve that account for a period of five years from the date of the claim made under Part VI of these Regulations. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The Commissioners may direct that registrable persons shall preserve the records specified in paragraph (1) above for a shorter period than that specified in this regulation; and such direction may be made so as to apply generally or in such cases as the Commissioners may stipulate. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In paragraph (1) above— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the reference to material being brought onto a landfill site is a reference to material that is brought onto the site for the purpose of a *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] disposal[^key-1c21784795a8861c9f56903b498b38ca] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the reference to material being removed from a landfill site is a reference to material being removed that has at some previous time fallen wholly or partly within paragraph (a) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-7162fdf3bb4ace5aac7b37aeeda67439][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 16ZZA (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Records: taxable activities at places other than landfill sites (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-2624fd1383cab3c9ca1895dd64b3c458][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Every registrable person (“RP”) carrying out taxable activities at a place other than a landfill site shall preserve the following records— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] their business and accounting records; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] their landfill tax account; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] transfer notes and any other original or copy records in relation to material brought onto or removed from the place other than a landfill site; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] all invoices (including landfill invoices) and similar documents issued to RP and copies of such invoices and similar documents issued by RP; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] all credit or debit notes or other documents received by RP which evidence an increase or decrease in the amount of any consideration for a disposal, and copies of such documents that are issued by RP; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
