- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] copies of any application made by RP to an environmental regulator in respect of a place other than a landfill site at which they carry out taxable activities; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] such other records as the Commissioners may specify in a notice published by them and not withdrawn by a further notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (3), every registrable person shall preserve the records specified in paragraph (1) above for a period of six years. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The Commissioners may direct that registrable persons shall preserve the records specified in paragraph (1) above for a shorter period than that specified in this regulation; and such direction may be made so as to apply generally or in such cases as the Commissioners may stipulate. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 16ZA (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Information relating to qualifying fines (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3ebbcff2c450c9fbd87de49ec18d6898][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where a registrable person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] carrying on taxable activities at a landfill site[^key-5c1b0752f51a2e5adb887c78b0900b86] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] has carried out a LOI test on any fines, that person must— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] retain a representative sample of at least one kilogram from the load of fines tested until the last working day of the month after the end of the quarter immediately following the quarter in which the LOI test was carried out; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] notify the Commissioners, by not later than the last working day of the month next after the end of the quarter in which the LOI test was carried out, of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any LOI test result in which the LOI percentage exceeds that specified in article 4 of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Landfill Tax (Qualifying Fines) (No. 2) Order 2015[^key-f6120aecbb5a2447f3752eb386a63d2c] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name of the person responsible for the waste treatment of the fines, and the address at which such treatment took place. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] If a person fails to comply with any requirement of paragraph (1) he shall be liable to a penalty of £250. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 16A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Information areas (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-660bd5d5763e646e4044e246bc6be094][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] An officer of Revenue and Customs is authorised to require a person to designate a part of a landfill site (an “information area”), and a person must designate an information area if so required. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where material at a landfill site is not going to be disposed of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-4964fe7c0ba8b7d617871f3f4e8988d0]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] and the Commissioners consider, or an officer of theirs considers, there to be a risk to the revenue— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the material must be deposited in an information area; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a registrable person must give the Commissioners, or an officer of theirs, information and maintain a record in accordance with paragraph 4 below. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A designation ceases to have effect if a notice in writing to that effect is given to a registrable person by the Commissioners or by an officer of Revenue and Customs. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A registrable person must maintain a record in relation to the information area of the following information, and give this information to the Commissioners or an officer of theirs if requested— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the weight and description of all material deposited there; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the intended destination or use of all such material and, where any material has been removed or used, the actual destination or use of that material; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the weight and description of any such material sorted or removed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART IV (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — CREDIT: GENERAL (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 17 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-7a37d6d923ba811abef84376ccf3f38c][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

In this Part— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- “relevant accounting period” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) in the case of an entitlement to credit arising under Part V of these Regulations, the accounting period in which the reuse condition or, as the case may be, the enforced removal condition was satisfied; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) in the case of an entitlement to credit arising under Part VI of these Regulations, the accounting period in which the period of one year from the date of the issue of the landfill invoice expired; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (c) in the case of an entitlement arising under Part VII of these Regulations, the accounting period in which the qualifying contribution was made; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “relevant amount” means the amount of the credit as determined in accordance with Part V, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VI *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VII *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] of these Regulations, as the case may be; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “relevant tax” means the tax, if any, that was required to have been paid as a condition of the entitlement to credit. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 18 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Scope (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-4d7a98b5e80de6ad74c33b8a8eb1ecf7][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] This Part applies to entitlements to credit *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in respect of disposals at a landfill site[^key-08025768f4dc2696d7f7004cef299354] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] arising under Part V, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VI *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] VII *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] of these Regulations. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] No credit arising under any provision of these Regulations may be claimed except in accordance with this Part. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 19 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Claims in returns (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-6b550e3185ece1f1d1b7d3ff229c52cf][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraphs (2) and (3) below, a person entitled to credit may claim it by deducting its amount from any tax due from him for the relevant accounting period or any subsequent accounting period and, where he does so, he shall make his return for that accounting period accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the entitlement to credit arises under Part VII of these Regulations paragraph (1) above shall apply as if there were substituted for “or any subsequent accounting period” the words “or any subsequent accounting period in the same contribution year as determined in relation to that person under regulation 31”. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The Commissioners may make directions generally or with regard to particular cases prescribing rules in accordance with which credit may or shall be held over to be credited in an accounting period subsequent to the relevant accounting period; and where such a direction has been made that credit, subject to any subsequent such direction varying or withdrawing the rules, may only be claimed in accordance with those rules. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 20 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Payments in respect of credit (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-faa80fc531f591cbef64b6f27fcfba78][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (5) below, where the total credit claimed by a registrable person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in respect of disposals at a landfill site[^key-301b99cc90b50c58721283aaa8becb30] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in accordance with this Part exceeds the total of the tax due from him for the accounting period, the Commissioners shall pay to him an amount equal to the excess. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the Commissioners have cancelled the registration of a person in accordance with section 47(6) of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], and he is not a registrable person, he shall make any claim in respect of credit to which this Part applies by making an application in writing. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person making an application under paragraph (2) above shall furnish to the Commissioners full particulars in relation to the credit claimed, including (but not restricted to)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] except in the case of an entitlement to credit arising under Part VII of these Regulations, the return in which the relevant tax was accounted for; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] except in the case of an entitlement to credit arising under Part VII of these Regulations, the amount of the tax and the date and manner of its payment; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the events by virtue of which the entitlement to credit arose. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (5) below, where the Commissioners are satisfied that a person who has made a claim in accordance with paragraphs (2) and (3) above is entitled to credit, and that he has not previously had the benefit of that credit, they shall pay to him an amount equal to the credit. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The Commissioners shall not be liable to make any payment under this regulation unless and until the person has made all the returns which he was required to make. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART V (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — CREDIT: PERMANENT REMOVALS (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) ETC. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 21 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Entitlement to credit (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-722fd754b9bdf7b7f861253c908d45d7][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] An entitlement to credit arises under this Part where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a registered person has accounted for an amount of tax and, except where the removal by virtue of which sub-paragraph (b) below is satisfied takes place in the accounting period in which credit arising under this Part is claimed in accordance with Part IV of these Regulations, he has paid that tax; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in relation to the disposal on which that tax was charged, either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the reuse condition has been satisfied; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the enforced removal condition has been satisfied. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The reuse condition is satisfied where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the disposal has been made with the intention that the material comprised in it— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] would be recycled or incinerated, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] removed for use (other than by way of a further disposal) at a place other than a relevant site; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or[^key-ac30e7c31b7963b0cc6ef1c929a8c721] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] removed for use in restoration of a relevant site and the material involved has previously been used to create or maintain temporary hard standing, to create or maintain a temporary screening bund or to create or maintain a temporary haul road; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that material, or some of it, has been recycled, incinerated or permanently removed from the landfill site, as the case may be, in accordance with that intention; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that recycling, incineration or removal— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] has taken place no later than one year after the date of the disposal; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] where water had been added to the material in order to facilitate its disposal, has taken place no later than five years after the date of the disposal; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the registered person has, before the disposal, notified the Commissioners in writing that he intends to make one or more removals of material in relation to which sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) above will be satisfied. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purpose of paragraph (2)(a)(ii) above a relevant site is the landfill site at which the disposal was made or any other landfill site. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The enforced removal condition is satisfied where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the disposal is in breach of the terms of the licence *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] , resolution or permit[^key-412fcc9c796a3c5b9eb2d3d600bfbf6b] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], as the case may be, by virtue of which the land constitutes a landfill site; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the registered person has been directed to remove the material comprised in the disposal, or some of it, by a relevant authority and he has removed it, or some of it; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a further *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-24ed2ca98170118d7eff4a4f1c8d1019]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] disposal of the material has been made *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] at a UK landfill site[^key-213acddb76db0a6dea34aa65007090d6] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] and, except where the registered person is the person liable for the tax chargeable on that further disposal, he has paid to the site operator an amount representing that tax. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purpose of paragraph (4)(b) above the following are relevant authorities— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Environment Agency; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (aa (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5c2184e80e356040a73b31da14f9f22b][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Natural Resources Body for Wales; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Scottish Environment Protection Agency; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Department of the Environment for Northern Ireland; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a district council in Northern Ireland. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The amount of the credit arising under this Part shall be equal to the tax that was charged on the disposal; except that where only some of the material comprised in that disposal is removed, the amount of the credit shall be such proportion of that tax as the material removed forms of the total of the material. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-7637b68d95f5be1742cf0c3e23e2a831][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In this Regulation— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- “disposal area” means any area of a landfill site where any disposal takes place; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “haul road” means any road within the landfill site which gives access to a disposal area; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “hard standing” means a base within a landfill site on which any landfill site activity such as sorting, treatment, processing, storage or recycling is carried out; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “screening bund” means any structure on a landfill site (whether below or above ground) put in place to protect or conceal any landfill site activity or to reduce nuisance from noise *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb];[^key-203c7f71d320cd9a9b491993b2561781] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “UK landfill site” means—[^key-dd72cfae24d21aca1347140cd51d13ba] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) a landfill site; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) a landfill site in respect of which there is an authorisation in force at the time of the disposal within the meaning of section 12(1) of the Landfill Tax (Scotland) Act 2014; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (c) an authorised landfill site within the meaning of section 5 of the Landfill Disposals Tax (Wales) Act 2017. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3c87c72b24d9cc82bcb613854e19e2de][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraph (4)(c) only, “disposal” shall be construed— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when that disposal takes place in England or Northern Ireland, in accordance with the definition in regulation 2(1); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in Scotland, in accordance with section 4 of the Landfill Tax (Scotland) Act 2014; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in Wales, in accordance with section 4 of the Landfill Disposals Tax (Wales) Act 2017. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART VI (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — CREDIT: BAD DEBTS (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 22 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-9ef3cf1d15b27717f9d65acf3dd15cf2][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

In this Part— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- “claim” means a claim in accordance with Part IV of these Regulations for an amount of credit arising under this Part and “claimant” shall be construed accordingly; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “customer” means a person for whom a taxable activity is carried out by the claimant; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “outstanding amount” means, in relation to any claim— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) if at the time of the claim the claimant has received no payment in respect of the amount written off in his accounts, the amount so written off; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) if at that time he has received a payment, the amount by which the amount written off exceeds the payment (or the aggregate of the payments); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “relevant disposal” means any taxable disposal upon which a claim is based; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “security” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (a) in relation to England, Wales and Northern Ireland, any mortgage, charge, lien or other security; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (b) in relation to Scotland, any security (whether heritable or moveable), any floating charge and any right of lien or preference and right of retention (other than a right of compensation or set-off). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 23 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Scope (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3b95e250b9d295960d006830c95572a5][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

An entitlement to credit arises under this Part where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a registered person has carried out a taxable activity for a consideration in money for a customer with whom he is not connected; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he has accounted for and paid tax on the disposal concerned; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the whole or any part of the consideration for the disposal has been written off in his accounts as a bad debt; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he has issued a landfill invoice in respect of the disposal which shows the amount of tax chargeable; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that invoice was issued— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] within 14 days of the date of the disposal, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] within such other period as may have been specified in a direction of the Commissioners made under section 61(3) of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a period of one year (beginning with the date of the issue of that invoice) has elapsed; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the following provisions of this Part have been complied with. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 24 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Amount of credit (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-b19a622e1db28f16dcebb410f97cf395][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

The credit arising under this Part shall be of an amount equal to such proportion of the tax charged on the relevant disposal as the outstanding amount forms of the total consideration. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 25 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Evidence required in support of claim (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-dd9d228207dec6aae7abdb99c41ce0eb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

The claimant, before he makes a claim, shall hold in respect of each relevant disposal— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a copy of the landfill invoice issued by him; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] records or any other documents showing that he has accounted for and paid tax on the disposal; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] records or any other documents showing that the consideration has been written off in his accounts as a bad debt. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 26 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Records required to be kept (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-02198cfab4c443d1c6903182b7a40c98][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any person who makes a claim shall make a record of that claim. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The record referred to in paragraph (1) above shall contain the following information in respect of each claim made: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in respect of each relevant disposal— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of tax charged; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the return in which that tax was accounted for and when it was paid; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date and identifying number of the landfill invoice that was issued; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iv (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any consideration that has been received (whether before the claim was made or subsequently); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (v (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the details of any transfer note; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the outstanding amount; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of the claim; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the return in which the claim was made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any records made in pursuance of this regulation shall be kept in a single account known as “the landfill tax bad debt account”. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 27 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Attribution of payments (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-62c84012eb0a279af97ea8e6be9145cf][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the claimant has carried out a taxable activity for a customer; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] there exist one or more other matters in respect of which the claimant is entitled to a debt owed by the customer (whether they involve a taxable disposal or not and whether they are connected with waste or not); and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a payment has been received by the claimant from the customer, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

the payment shall be attributed to the taxable activity and the other matters in accordance with the rule set out in paragraphs (2) and (3) below (and the debts arising in respect of the taxable activity and the other matters are collectively referred to in those paragraphs as debts). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The payment shall be attributed to the debt which arose earliest and, if not wholly attributed to that debt, thereafter to debts in the order of the dates on which they arose, except that attribution under this paragraph shall not be made if the payment was allocated to a debt by the customer at the time of payment and the debt was paid in full. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the earliest debt and the other debts to which the whole of the payment could be attributed arose on the same day; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the debts to which the balance of the payment could be attributed in accordance with paragraph (2) above arose on the same day, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

the payment shall be attributed to those debts by multiplying, for each such debt, the payment made by a fraction of which the numerator is the amount remaining unpaid in respect of that debt and the denominator is the amount remaining unpaid in respect of all those debts. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 28 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Repayment of credit (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-ba0e57e35cd04b149e90e0a4aa464e9c][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where a claimant— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] has benefited from an amount of credit to which he was entitled under this Part; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a payment for the relevant disposal is subsequently received; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a payment is, by virtue of regulation 27, treated as attributed to the relevant disposal, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  he shall repay to the Commissioners such amount as equals the amount of the credit, or the balance thereof, multiplied by a fraction of which the numerator is the amount so received or attributed, and the denominator is the amount of the outstanding consideration. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the claimant— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] fails to comply with the requirements of regulation 26; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in relation to the documents mentioned in that regulation, fails to comply with either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] regulation 16; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any obligation arising under paragraph 3 of Schedule 5 to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  he shall repay to the Commissioners the amount of the claim to which the failure to comply relates. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 29 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Writing off debts (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-5fa6ee7e750e2cddb5d6c034b9f48a5e][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] This regulation shall apply for the purpose of determining whether, and to what extent, the consideration is to be taken to have been written off as a bad debt. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The whole or any part of the consideration for a taxable activity shall be taken to have been written off as a bad debt where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the claimant has written it off in his accounts as a bad debt; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he has made an entry in relation to that activity in the landfill tax bad debt account in accordance with regulation 26 (and this shall apply regardless of whether a claim can be made in relation to that activity at that time). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the claimant owes an amount of money to the customer which can be set off, the consideration written off in the landfill tax bad debt account shall be reduced by the amount so owed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the claimant holds in relation to the customer an enforceable security, the consideration written off in the landfill tax bad debt account shall be reduced by the value of the security. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART VII (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — CREDIT: BODIES CONCERNED WITH THE ENVIRONMENT (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 30 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Interpretation and general provisions (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3776e33a280982b9c34612e97b6dd6a2][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In this Part— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- “approved body” means a body approved for the time being under regulation 34; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “approved object” has the meaning given in regulation 33; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “contributing third party” means a person who has made or agreed to make (whether or not under a legally binding agreement) a payment to a registered person to secure the making by him of a qualifying contribution or to reimburse him, in whole or in part, for any such contribution he has made;[^key-a8be44dc2f8dff7039ca3adaa13369d9] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “income” includes interest[^key-276717a3c16768376d30421d480ea482] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] , other than interest arising from the investment of an investment contribution[^key-b717d94ad5e5495c93e54add9f4ec65d] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - the proportion of any proceeds attributable to the initial acquisition and disposal of an asset (or part of an asset) purchased with a qualifying contribution (or part of a qualifying contribution), or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - the proportion of any proceeds attributable to any subsequent acquisition and disposal of an asset (or part of an asset) purchased with the proceeds (or part of the proceeds) of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
    - the initial acquisition and disposal representing the original qualifying contributions, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
    - any subsequent acquisition and disposal representing the original qualifying contributions, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

    through any number of transactions. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “investment contribution” means a payment made to an approved body subject to a condition that it may only be invested for the purposes of generating interest;[^key-918ccce9a2666216809deb54e020a57a] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “qualifying contribution” has the meaning given in regulation 32; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “the regulatory body” means such body, if any, as in relation to which an approval of the Commissioners under regulation 35 has effect for the time being; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- “running costs” includes any cost incurred in connection with the management and administration of a body or its assets. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A body shall only be taken to spend a qualifying contribution in the course or furtherance of its approved objects— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in a case where the contribution is made subject to a condition that it may only be invested for the purpose of generating income, where the body so spends all of that income; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in a case not falling within sub-paragraph (a) above, where the body becomes entitled to income, where it so spends both the whole of the qualifying contribution and all of that income; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in a case not falling within either of sub-paragraphs (a) and (b) above, where the body so spends the whole of the qualifying contribution; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it transfers any qualifying contribution or income derived therefrom to another approved body, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that transfer is subject to a condition that the sum transferred shall be spent only in the course or furtherance of that other body’s approved objects. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Any approval, or revocation of such approval, by the Commissioners or the regulatory body shall be given by notice in writing to the body affected and shall take effect from the date the notice is given or such later date as the Commissioners or, as the case may be, the regulatory body may specify in it. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 31 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Entitlement to credit (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-35fff8c785e1db81ef5d5378d69506ad][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to the following provisions of this regulation, an entitlement to credit arises under this Part in respect of qualifying contributions made by registered persons. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (3) below, a person shall be entitled to credit in respect of 90 per cent. of the amount of each qualifying contribution made by him in any accounting period; and for this purpose a qualifying contribution made— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-33522701c5053ad1ff6bfe321efa7e42][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in one accounting period; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] before the return for the previous accounting period has been made; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] before the period within which that return is required to be made has expired, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

shall be treated as having been made in the accounting period mentioned in sub-paragraph (b) above (and not in the accounting period in which it was in fact made). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In respect of the qualifying contributions made in each contribution year, a person shall not be entitled to credit of an amount greater than *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] 5.3[^key-57260f6a9fc44a49735b666b2528b9b9] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] per cent. of his relevant tax liability. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-910a32eadee1a2a99230f61068a7e9ed][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purpose of paragraphs (2) and (3) the contribution year of a person is his first contribution year and then each period of 12 months beginning on 1st April. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-9b48a2e9a64442f81a311528d9b13953][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The reference in paragraph (4) to the first contribution year of a person is a reference to the period beginning with his effective date of registration and ending on the day immediately preceding the first day of the next contribution year. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-a0d3b6bca2107f22089b12881f536fe7][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where one contribution year ends and another contribution year begins in an accounting period, the amount of any qualifying contribution which, by virtue of paragraph (2), is treated as made in that period shall be apportioned, in accordance with paragraph (6A), between those contribution years. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The apportionment shall be on the basis of either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the number of days of the accounting period that fall before 1st April and the number of days that fall on and after that day; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of tax charged on taxable disposals made in the accounting period before 1st April and the amount of tax charged on taxable disposals made in that period on and after that day, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

whichever the registered person may choose. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-507fc9434782092fa3e99fcfffdaa7c1][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6D (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-507fc9434782092fa3e99fcfffdaa7c1][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6E (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-507fc9434782092fa3e99fcfffdaa7c1][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] paragraph (10)[^key-d7dd4c7b0855d17701a63cf14aa72c8a] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] below, the reference in paragraph (3) above to the relevant tax liability of a person is a reference to the aggregate of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the tax payable by him, if any, in respect of the accounting period in relation to which that liability falls to be determined; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the tax payable by him, if any, in respect of any earlier accounting period or periods which fall within the same contribution year as that accounting period; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

and where in respect of any accounting period he is entitled to a payment under regulation 20 the aggregate of the tax payable by him in respect of the accounting periods mentioned in sub-paragraphs (a) and (b) above shall be reduced by the amount of that payment. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 32 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Qualifying contributions (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-b717aadc1a1e3175908f690e5677a929][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (1A), a payment is a qualifying contribution if[^key-1b496d487124e43086d49793b36c1301] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is made by a registered person to an approved body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is made subject to a condition that the body shall spend the sum paid or any income derived from it or both only in the course or furtherance of its approved objects; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the requirements of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] paragraphs (2) to (2B)[^key-23130d19e395480611230db183dbb5a3] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] below have been complied with in relation to that payment; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is not repaid to him *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] , or a contributing third party,[^key-2bde69ebf35530ea9cf7a99ed2e2fbbf] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the same accounting period as that in which it was made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d904f8ab1da2b1185f8e4df5d400e05e][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A payment is not a qualifying contribution if it is an investment contribution. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person claiming credit arising under this Part shall make a record containing the following information— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount and date of each payment he has made to an approved body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and enrolment number of that body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-285ff2913a0d019f8d68909d2912a907][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and address of any contributing third party; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of the payment made or to be made by the contributing third party and the date, or as the case may require, dates on which payment of the whole or any part of that amount— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] was received, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] is expected to be received *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-f57c5fdfd07158abf4c0ead868f22baf][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person claiming credit under this Part for a contribution in relation to which there is a contributing third party shall have provided to the regulatory body or, if they are performing the functions specified in regulation 34(1) below, to the Commissioners the following information— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and address of the contributing third party; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of the payment made or to be made by the contributing third party and the date, or as the case may require, dates on which payment of the whole or any part of that amount— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] was received, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] is expected to be received; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the enrolment number of the approved body to whom the contribution was made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person claiming credit under this Part for a contribution in relation to which there is a contributing third party shall have informed the approved body to which the contribution is made of the name and address of the contributing third party. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-76faccfac67c418bb7c5d23ee58182ca][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of this Part where any qualifying contribution or income derived therefrom is transferred to a body as described in regulation 30(2)(d)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the body to whom the sum is transferred shall be treated as having received qualifying contributions of the amount concerned; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that body shall be treated as having received those qualifying contributions from the registered person or persons who originally paid them (but this shall not give rise to any further entitlement to credit in respect of those contributions). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 33 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Bodies eligible for approval (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-9c98b268067fe414a040427b639b9750][^M_E_9f0e5394-aaa0-4d8e-fbde-e5acd34dbdbc][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

*Extent: E+W+N.I.*

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A body is eligible to be approved if—[^key-0451d416c199825868155dbdd86756ba] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a body corporate, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a trust, partnership or other unincorporated body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] its objects are or include any of the objects within paragraph (2) below (approved objects); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is precluded from distributing and does not distribute any profit it makes or other income it receives; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it applies any profit or other income to the furtherance of its objects (whether or not approved objects); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-4301b64b0a9ef0b91358c6e202f2f15b][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is precluded from applying any of its funds for the benefit of any of the persons— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] who have made qualifying contributions to it, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] who were a contributing third party in relation to such contributions, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  except that such persons may benefit where they belong to a class of persons that benefits generally; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-6716a7cb0cc6a0734fb3b5cfbd9d3b46]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d61a59d01d88d37860425842a2cffd8b][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is not controlled by one or more of the persons and bodies listed in paragraphs (1A) and (1B) below; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] none of the persons or bodies listed in paragraph (1B) below is concerned in its management; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (h (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it pays to the regulatory body an application fee of £100 or such lesser sum as the regulatory body may require. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8d2691ec5b41de28b6bc63e531b66e9a][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The persons and bodies mentioned in paragraph (1)(f) above are: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a local authority; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a body corporate controlled by one or more local authorities; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a registered person; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person connected with any of the persons or bodies mentioned in sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The persons and bodies mentioned in paragraph 1(f) and (g) above are: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person who controlled or was concerned in the management of a body the approval of which was revoked otherwise than under regulation 34(1)(ee); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person who has been convicted of an indictable offence; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person who is disqualified *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] from being a charity trustee or a trustee for a charity by virtue of section 178 of the Charities Act 2011[^key-cc12b539fbaa6cf0f9368c8074332c60] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person connected with any of the persons or bodies mentioned in sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) above; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person who is incapable by reason of mental disorder. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purpose of paragraph 1(B)(e) above, a person shall be treated as incapable by reason of mental disorder where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5cd5f39a849314793c2b551ab48247de][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in England and Wales, the person lacks capacity within the meaning of the Mental Capacity Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [2005 (c.9) *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2005/9)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to administer and manage his property and affairs; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in Scotland, the court has appointed a curator bonis, tutor or judicial factor; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in Northern Ireland, the court has exercised any of its powers under Part VIII of the Mental Health (Northern Ireland) Order 1986 (whether or not by virtue of Article 97(2) of that Order), *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but shall cease to be so treated where the judge or court concerned has made a finding that he is not or is no longer incapable of managing and administering his property and affairs. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The objects of a body are approved objects insofar as they are any of the following objects— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in relation to any land the use of which for any economic, social or environmental purpose has been prevented or restricted because of the carrying on of an activity on the land which has ceased— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] reclamation, remediation or restoration; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any other operation intended to facilitate economic, social or environmental use; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but this is subject to paragraph (3) below; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in relation to any land the condition of which, by reason of the carrying on of an activity on the land which has ceased, is such that pollution (whether of that land or not) is being or may be caused— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any operation intended to prevent or reduce any potential for pollution; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any operation intended to remedy or mitigate the effects of any pollution that has been caused, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but this is subject to paragraph (3) below; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
