- (cc (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-ab0af74f9095b10b69b7f081e06b01c3][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] where it is for the protection of the environment, the provision, maintenance or improvement of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a public park; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] another public amenity, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  in the vicinity of a landfill site, provided the conditions in paragraph (6) below are satisfied; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the provision, conservation, restoration or enhancement of a natural habitat; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(10 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraphs (3) *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] , (3A)[^key-8c7820132988f59aa48be50440083f80] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] and (6) above a condition is relevant if it is— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a condition of any planning permission or other statutory consent or approval granted on the application of any person making a qualifying contribution to the body, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-84b3b85424e9efc82e8e2869d05c78cb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a term of an agreement made under— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] section 106 of the Town and Country Planning Act 1990, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] section 75 of the Town and Country Planning (Scotland) Act 1997, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] article 40 of the Planning (Northern Ireland) Order 1991, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  to which such a person is a party. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(11 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-aa6418068466861f02baf4f2a5bcea56][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] An object shall only be regarded as falling within paragraph (2) above if the work involved is carried out in England *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-ee64c374b67791508593b9fc728481ea]... or Northern Ireland. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(12 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraph (11) work is to be regarded as being carried out either— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the place where, (as the case may be), the land, water, public park or public amenity or building or other structure referred to in sub-paragraphs *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] (2)(a) to (e)[^key-0c8ef6d04672c1f9fe44191d74c531bb] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] is located, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of land, water, a public park or public amenity which comprises an area of which a proportion is located in England *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-c5d86d257a3ea354bb482b425ce2e004]... or Northern Ireland, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] and a proportion is located in another part of the United Kingdom,[^key-ef56e4c1dfbd6ad06ba6353d5812f6e1] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the place where the largest proportion of that area is located, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of a building or other structure of which a proportion is located in England *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-6e4e7adc0776bbcf5130016ae26ef0cc]... or Northern Ireland, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] and a proportion is located in another part of the United Kingdom,[^key-378d50ea292934bb45809d117d02dc20] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the place where the largest proportion of that building or other structure is located. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 33. Bodies eligible for approval (affected by repeal, see note)[^V_S_bdf99d87-fd0a-bf16-bb29-61cee76bcf82][^M_E_118dd6ac-31c2-460b-c104-5e4f7d78c29f][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

*Extent: S*

(1) A body is eligible to be approved if—[^V_S_e7e19386-e064-bcdc-ea19-79943b9f5410] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) it is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) a body corporate, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a trust, partnership or other unincorporated body; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) its objects are or include any of the objects within paragraph (2) below (approved objects); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) it is precluded from distributing and does not distribute any profit it makes or other income it receives; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d) it applies any profit or other income to the furtherance of its objects (whether or not approved objects); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e)[^V_S_0eaccbb1-ce1a-e60e-d04e-92abdaf4c5c6] it is precluded from applying any of its funds for the benefit of any of the persons— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) who have made qualifying contributions to it, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] who were a contributing third party in relation to such contributions, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  except that such persons may benefit where they belong to a class of persons that benefits generally; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^V_S_9df07cce-840a-3eeb-f379-ff7ced16efbc]...
- (f)[^V_S_b7ca84c0-bba1-001d-33ea-e11cf06c5e1d] it is not controlled by one or more of the persons and bodies listed in paragraphs (1A) and (1B) below; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g) none of the persons or bodies listed in paragraph (1B) below is concerned in its management; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (h) it pays to the regulatory body an application fee of £100 or such lesser sum as the regulatory body may require. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1A)[^V_S_96949097-1dbd-1375-677c-993068aebca9] The persons and bodies mentioned in paragraph (1)(f) above are: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) a local authority; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) a body corporate controlled by one or more local authorities; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) a registered person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d) a person connected with any of the persons or bodies mentioned in sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) above. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1B) The persons and bodies mentioned in paragraph 1(f) and (g) above are: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) a person who controlled or was concerned in the management of a body the approval of which was revoked otherwise than under regulation 34(1)(ee); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) a person who has been convicted of an indictable offence; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) a person who is disqualified *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] from being a charity trustee or a trustee for a charity by virtue of section 178 of the Charities Act 2011 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb];
- (d) a person connected with any of the persons or bodies mentioned in sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) above; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e) a person who is incapable by reason of mental disorder. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1C) For the purpose of paragraph 1(B)(e) above, a person shall be treated as incapable by reason of mental disorder where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a)[^V_S_53198027-343f-3ed1-bf96-9c1eb9510cef] in England and Wales, the person lacks capacity within the meaning of the Mental Capacity Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [2005 (c.9)](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/ukpga/2005/9) to administer and manage his property and affairs; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) in Scotland, the court has appointed a curator bonis, tutor or judicial factor; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) in Northern Ireland, the court has exercised any of its powers under Part VIII of the Mental Health (Northern Ireland) Order 1986 (whether or not by virtue of Article 97(2) of that Order), *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but shall cease to be so treated where the judge or court concerned has made a finding that he is not or is no longer incapable of managing and administering his property and affairs. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2) The objects of a body are approved objects insofar as they are any of the following objects— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) in relation to any land the use of which for any economic, social or environmental purpose has been prevented or restricted because of the carrying on of an activity on the land which has ceased— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) reclamation, remediation or restoration; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any other operation intended to facilitate economic, social or environmental use; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but this is subject to paragraph (3) below; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (b) in relation to any land the condition of which, by reason of the carrying on of an activity on the land which has ceased, is such that pollution (whether of that land or not) is being or may be caused— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) any operation intended to prevent or reduce any potential for pollution; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any operation intended to remedy or mitigate the effects of any pollution that has been caused, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

but this is subject to paragraph (3) below; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (c)[^V_S_bd611c65-38ba-3283-0ec0-2f8ae96cdce3] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (cc (affected by repeal, see note))[^V_S_6ff4b898-bdcf-ec7a-4387-90928bb7b842][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (d) where it is for the protection of the environment, the provision, maintenance or improvement of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) a public park; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] another public amenity, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  in the vicinity of a landfill site, provided the conditions in paragraph (6) below are satisfied; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (da (affected by repeal, see note))[^V_S_d0c456a2-8b9d-7ec3-1ff8-3de3ba4f88c9][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] where it is for the protection of the environment, and subject to paragraph (3A) below, the conservation or promotion of biological diversity through– *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) the provision, conservation, restoration or enhancement of a natural habitat; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the maintenance or recovery of a species in its natural habitat, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  on land or in water situated in the vicinity of a landfill site; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e) where it is for the protection of the environment, the maintenance, repair or restoration of a building or other structure which— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) is a place of religious worship or of historic or architectural interest, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] is open to the public, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] is situated in the vicinity of a landfill site, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  provided the conditions in paragraph (6) below are satisfied; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f) the provision of financial, administration and other similar services to bodies which are within this regulation and only such bodies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2A)[^V_S_e65c63f1-7b93-e17d-4fe3-94f0b9dccac9] In paragraph (2)(da) above “biological diversity” has the same meaning as in the United Nations Environmental Programme Convention on Biological Diversity of 1992. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3) An object shall not be, or shall no longer be, regarded as falling within paragraph (2)(a) or (b) above if the reclamation, remediation, restoration or other operation— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) is such that any benefit from it will accrue to any person who has carried out or knowingly permitted the activity which has ceased; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) involves works which are required to be carried out by a notice or order within paragraph (4) below; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) is wholly or partly required to be carried out by a relevant condition. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3A)[^V_S_92110051-00f5-41aa-a8e4-d97cbc3bac2d] An object shall not be, or shall no longer be, regarded as falling within paragraph (2)(da) above if it involves works which– *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) are required to be carried out by a notice or order within paragraph (4) below; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) are required to be carried out in accordance with an agreement made under section 16 of the National Parks and Access to the Countryside Act 1949; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) are required to be carried out in accordance with an agreement made under section 15 of the Countryside Act 1968; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d) give effect to any provision of a management scheme under section 28J of the Wildlife and Countryside Act 1981 or are required to be carried out by a notice served under section 28K of that Act; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e) are wholly or partly required to be carried out by a relevant condition; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f) are carried out with a view to profit. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4) The notices and order mentioned in paragraph (3) above are— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) a works notice served under section 46A of the Control of Pollution Act 1974 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00004];
- (b) an enforcement notice served under section 13 of the Environmental Protection Act 1990 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00005];
- (c) a prohibition notice served under section 14 of the Environmental Protection Act 1990; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d) an order under section 26 of the Environmental Protection Act 1990; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e) a remediation notice served under section 78E of the Environmental Protection Act 1990 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00006];
- (f) an enforcement notice served under section 90B of the Water Resources Act 1991 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00007];
- (g) a works notice served under section 161A of the Water Resources Act 1991 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00008];
- (h)[^key-9f20cef90d39334c17392a0a9d6f6e7e] a regulatory notice, surrender notice or revocation notice issued under the Environmental Authorisations (Scotland) Regulations 2018, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (i) an order granted under regulation 75 of the Environmental Authorisations (Scotland) Regulations 2018. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (i)[^key-ec32efa033c334799297402b4a9c1d56] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (j)[^key-ec32efa033c334799297402b4a9c1d56] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(5)[^V_S_5945ebb9-aa4b-8845-5969-ea2e5d0a53bd] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(6) The conditions mentioned in sub-paragraphs (d) and (e) of paragraph (2) above are— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) in a case falling within sub-paragraph (d), that the provision of the park or amenity is not required by a relevant condition; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) in a case falling within either of those sub-paragraphs, that the park, amenity, building or structure (as the case may be) is not to be operated with a view to profit. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7) Where the objects of a body are or include any of the objects set out in paragraph (2) above, the following shall also be regarded as objects within that paragraph— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) the use of qualifying contributions in paying the running costs of the body, but this is subject to paragraph (8) below; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) [^V_S_bbc3f1d3-7168-845e-adbf-7437d69bd6cc]... the use of qualifying contributions in paying a contribution to the running costs of the regulatory body. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(8) The use of qualifying contributions in paying the running costs of the body shall only be regarded as an approved object if the body determines so to use no more than such proportion of the total of qualifying contributions, together with any income derived from them, (or, in the case of a contribution within regulation 30(2)(a), only that income) as the proportion of that total forms of the total funds at its disposal and does not in fact use a greater amount. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(9) For the purposes of paragraph (1) above *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a body or person (in either case, for the purposes of this paragraph, “the person”)[^V_S_2d2d03fb-6df8-4a8c-8a33-4ebd38d8e322] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall be taken to control a body where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) in the case of a body which is a body corporate, the person is empowered by statute to control that body’s activities or if he is that body’s holding company within the meaning of section *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] 1159 of and Schedule 6 to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Companies Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] 2006, and an individual shall be taken to control a body corporate if he, were he a company, would be that body’s holding company within the meaning of that Act; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) in the case of a body which is a trust or a partnership, where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) the person, taken together with any nominee of his, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any nominee of the person, taken together with any nominee of that nominee or any other nominee of the person, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  forms a majority of the total number of trustees or partners, as the case may be; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) in the case of any other body, where the person, whether directly or through any nominee, has the power— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) to appoint or remove any officer of the body; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to determine the objects of the body; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] to determine how any of the body’s funds may be applied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(10) For the purposes of paragraphs (3) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] , (3A)[^V_S_69bdeaa4-9530-063b-3986-dbe388aa432d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] and (6) above a condition is relevant if it is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) a condition of any planning permission or other statutory consent or approval granted on the application of any person making a qualifying contribution to the body, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b)[^V_S_9c28857a-fa6f-c8e3-e7dc-e623ae0bee88] a term of an agreement made under— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i) section 106 of the Town and Country Planning Act 1990, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] section 75 of the Town and Country Planning (Scotland) Act 1997, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] article 40 of the Planning (Northern Ireland) Order 1991, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  to which such a person is a party. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(11) An object shall only be regarded as falling within paragraph (2) above if the work involved is carried out in England, Wales, or Northern Ireland. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(12) For the purposes of paragraph (11) work is to be regarded as being carried out either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a) in the place where, (as the case may be), the land, water, public park or public amenity or building or other structure referred to in sub-paragraphs (2)(a)-(f) is located, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b) in the case of land, water, a public park or public amenity which comprises an area of which a proportion is located in England, Wales or Northern Ireland, the place where the largest proportion of that area is located, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c) in the case of a building or other structure of which a proportion is located in England, Wales, or Northern Ireland, the place where the largest proportion of that building or other structure is located. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 33A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Obligations of approved bodies (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-871f0e8f355400a59d65c1bc865880bb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] An approved body shall— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] continue to meet all the requirements of regulation 33 above; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (aa (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-12edc9113da33ecfbd7cb3767121a672][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] comply with such conditions as the regulatory body may impose from time to time under regulation 34(1)(aa) (including any conditions varied under regulation 34(1)(ab)); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] apply qualifying contributions and any income derived therefrom only to approved objects; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] not apply any of its funds for the benefit of any of the persons who have made qualifying contributions to it or who were contributing third parties in relation to such contributions (except to the extent that they benefit by virtue of belonging to a class of persons that benefits generally); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] make a record of each of the following matters and retain that record for the record retention period[^key-9ce5a9461b67fa4acbc6bb87f10b7acb] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name, address and registration number of each registered person making a qualifying contribution to the body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and address of any contributing third party in relation to a qualifying contribution received by the body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount and date of receipt of each qualifying contribution and the amount and date of receipt of any income derived therefrom; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iv (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of a transfer of the whole or part of any qualifying contribution or income derived therefrom to or from the body, the date of the transfer, the amount transferred, the name and enrolment number of the body from or, as the case may require, to which it was transferred, the name, address and registration number of the person who made the qualifying contribution and the name and address of any contributing third party in relation to the qualifying contribution; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (v (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in respect of each qualifying contribution and any income derived therefrom, including any such amount transferred to the body by another approved body, the date of and all other details relating to its expenditure; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] provide the following information to the regulatory body or, if they are performing the functions specified in regulation 34(1) below, to the Commissioners within 7 days of the receipt by it of any qualifying contribution— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of the contribution; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date it was received; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and registration number of the person making the contribution; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iv (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and address of any contributing third party in relation to the contribution notified to it by virtue of regulation 32(2B) above; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] notify the regulatory body within seven days of any *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] transfer by that approved body[^key-ac857fe435ecbf0cb333b3983817b938] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] of qualifying contributions or of income derived therefrom of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date of the transfer; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the enrolment number of the approved body by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-cd4e2d0a9768ec0cb0ab554cbd6ff757]... which the transfer was made; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount transferred; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iv (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and registration number of the person who made the qualifying contribution; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (v (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and address of any contributing third party in relation to the contribution; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (vi (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the approved objects to which the transferred funds are to be applied; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-ce89cb0bff5605ab19461fc073abc0ba][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a request for such information or access made during the record retention period *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (h (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] submit to the regulatory body or, if they are performing the functions specified in regulation 34(1) below, to the Commissioners within *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] 28[^M_F_a4617b2f-200b-4de3-82ce-ea064d879e25] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] days of the end of the relevant period determined in accordance with paragraph (2) below details of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] qualifying contributions and any other income or profit whatsoever received by it, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any expenditure made by it during the period, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any balances held by it at the end of the period; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (ha (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-dbf40e1430a8ad36bf143b9f7db02172][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] submit to the regulatory body at its request, not later than the day specified in paragraph (3), so many of the following details as it requires at any time during the relevant period— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] qualifying contributions and any other income or profit whatsoever received by it during the period, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any expenditure made by it during the period, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any balances held during the period; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (hb (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] if the Commissioners are performing the functions specified in regulation 34(1) instead of the regulatory body, submit, at their request, not later than the day specified in paragraph (3), so many of the following details as they may require at any time during the relevant period— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] qualifying contributions and any other income or profit whatsoever received by it during the period, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any expenditure made by it during the period, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any balances held during the period; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-46692b74e31ff16913616e8edb9838ae][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] submit to the regulatory body at its request, not later than the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] fourteenth day following the day on which the request is made[^M_F_0619455c-3617-422a-83a1-01837a6f1409] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb], independently audited financial accounts for the approved body’s last financial year; but such a request may not be made earlier than 10 months following the end of that financial year; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (j (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] pay to the regulatory body an amount equal to 5 per cent. of each qualifying contribution it receives, or such lesser amount as the regulatory body may require, towards its running costs within 14 days of receipt of a demand for payment. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-76e68a9db71133019000761b64103d8c][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(h), (1)(ha) and (1hb) the relevant period in respect of an approved body is— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of the first such period, the period commencing with the date on which the body was approved and ending on the following 31st March; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of subsequent periods, the period of 12 months commencing with the day after the end of the first or, as the case may require, a subsequent period. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5c1ec121bb7e9291b2cfca6b49e10798][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The day referred to in paragraphs *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] (1)(ha) and (1)(hb) is the twenty-eighth[^M_F_48cfca11-f553-48fa-c6ea-aa2e57dde6bf] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] day following the day on which a request is made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where an approved body submits details in accordance with a request made under paragraph (1)(ha) or paragraph (1)(hb) the requirement in paragraph (1)(h) shall not apply in respect of those details. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-6cfe11e13b4f53d711bfddcffe8df1f4][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In paragraph (1)(d) and (g), the “record retention period” is the period of six years beginning with the day on which the record is made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 34 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Functions of the regulatory body (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-6dc26250680655f9339494a902fb5ef6][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The regulatory body— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-f3185620d44de92d2cb20ae4740c5d1a][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall, on application being made to it by a body which is eligible to be approved under regulation 33 above, approve that body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (aa (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-9d72859f2e210f36c36286651bd4bab0][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] may— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] at the time a body is approved, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] subsequently, by notice delivered to that body, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  impose such conditions as it sees fit; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (ab (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] may, by notice delivered to a body, vary or revoke any condition of the approval; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-dc5ccec05b0fc0f2b880af9e6fa7f0e8][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-dc5ccec05b0fc0f2b880af9e6fa7f0e8][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-dc5ccec05b0fc0f2b880af9e6fa7f0e8][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_c2b13f7d-92a3-4615-a8a3-8c6f0fff90a9][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (ee (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-41ae3a76699e2c8e91d5830ec766f00c][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall revoke the approval of any body which applies for its approval to be revoked; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall maintain a roll of bodies which it has approved; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall allocate an identifying number (the enrolment number) to each such body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (h (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall remove from the roll any body whose approval *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] has been revoked under sub-paragraph (ee) or regulation 35(1)(h)[^M_F_be2a9c3a-821c-402b-ea98-09029e1fe8d5] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall satisfy itself, by reference to such records or other documents or information it thinks fit, that the qualifying contributions received by the body have been spent by it only in the course or furtherance of its approved objects; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-a03017a244fe962b50a1acd977b19dd9]... *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (k (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall, when notified by an approved body of the transfer to or by it of the whole or part of a qualifying contribution or of income derived therefrom, notify the registered person who made the qualifying contribution, and any contributing third party in relation to it, of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date of the transfer, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and enrolment number of the body by or, as the case may require, to whom the transfer was made; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (l (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0ec819cff9e80587477778b8f8704f24][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall comply with such conditions as the Commissioners may impose from time to time under regulation 35(1)(aa) (including any conditions varied under regulation 35(1)(ab)). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The Commissioners— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] may revoke the approval; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall not approve a body without first revoking the approval for any other body with effect from a time earlier than that for which the new approval is to take effect; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] for any time as regards which no approval has effect, may perform any of the functions specified in regulation 34(1); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] having regard to any information received from the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] regulatory *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] body, may serve notices *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] under regulation 36; and[^M_F_d419d2ed-3f69-4256-f4c1-d4a49cee2973] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (h (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] may, with respect to the approval of a body approved under regulation 34(1)(a), revoke that approval if the approved body fails to comply with any requirement of regulation 33A(1).[^M_F_6b8b61ca-42cb-4038-9145-1149f3a2370b] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 36 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Repayment of credit (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-f569eaf25c6710da6f435b923f2eb201][^key-c6034e839173e9c92c80573b4f3e70b3][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where a person has benefited from an amount of credit to which he was entitled under this Part and the Commissioners serve upon him a notice in relation to a qualifying contribution paid to an approved body— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] specifying that— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] they are not satisfied that the contribution has been spent by the body only in the course or furtherance of its approved objects; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] they are not satisfied that any income derived from the contribution has been so spent by the body; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] specifying a breach of a condition to which the approval of the body was made subject and which occurred before the contribution was spent by the body; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] specifying that— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the approval of the body has been revoked; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the contribution had not been spent by the body before that revocation took effect, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  he shall repay to the Commissioners the credit claimed in respect of the qualifying contribution. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purpose of paragraph (1) above where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] repayment is required in relation to credit that has been claimed in respect of more than one qualifying contribution in an accounting period; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] regulation 31(3) applied so that the amount of credit was restricted, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

the person shall be deemed to have claimed credit in respect of such proportion of each contribution made in that accounting period as the total credit claimed in accordance with that regulation forms of the total of the contributions made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-98ce6ecec575e5f96dc62430afb7ce19][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person has benefited from an amount of credit to which he was entitled under this Part; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the whole or a part of the qualifying contribution in respect of which the entitlement to credit arose has been repaid to him or a person who was a contributing third party in relation to the qualifying contribution, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

he shall pay to the Commissioners an amount equal to 90 per cent. of the amount repaid to him or, as the case may require, to the contributing third party. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Paragraph (5) below applies where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person has benefited from an amount of credit to which he was entitled under this Part; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he is entitled to a payment under regulation 20 in respect of a later accounting period in the same contribution year as the accounting period in respect of which that credit was claimed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where this paragraph applies the person shall pay to the Commissioners an amount equal to the difference between— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the aggregate of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of the credit from which he has benefited, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any other amounts of credit arising under this Part which he is or was entitled to claim, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  in respect of that contribution year; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the amount of credit which he would have been entitled to claim if he had in fact claimed the aggregate amount mentioned in sub-paragraph (a) above in the return for the accounting period in respect of which he was entitled to the payment under regulation 20. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person has benefited from an amount of credit to which he was entitled under this Part; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] he acquires an asset from a body to which he has made a qualifying contribution for— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] no consideration, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a consideration which is less than the open market value of the asset, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  he shall pay to the Commissioners an amount equal to 90 per cent. of the amount by which the open market value exceeds the consideration; but this is subject to paragraph (7) below. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A person required to pay an amount to the Commissioners by paragraph (6) above— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall not be required to pay more than the total amount of relevant credit; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] shall not be entitled to claim any further amounts of credit in respect of qualifying contributions made by him to the body in question on or after the date on which he acquired the asset. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraphs (6) and (7) above— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] “asset” includes land, goods or services and any interest in any of these; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the open market value of an asset is the amount of the consideration in money that would be payable for the asset by a person standing in no such relationship with any person as would affect that consideration; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] “relevant credit” means credit arising under this Part— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] from which a person has benefited, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] which has arisen in respect of qualifying contributions made by him to the body in question or treated by virtue of regulation 32(3) as having been received by that body from him. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART VIII (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — LANDFILL INVOICES (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 37 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Contents of a landfill invoice (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-b958e24bd759261558e9276f5bb8f1aa][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] An invoice is a landfill invoice if it contains the following information: *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] an identifying number; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date of its issue; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the date of the disposal or disposals in respect of which it is issued or, where a series of disposals is made for the same person, the dates between which the disposals were made; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name, address and registration number of the person issuing it; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the name and address of the person to whom it is issued; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the weight of the material disposed of; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (g (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a description of the material disposed of; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (h (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the rate of tax chargeable in relation to the disposal or, if the invoice relates to more than one disposal and the rate of tax for each of them is not the same, the rate of tax chargeable for each disposal; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the total amount payable for which the invoice is issued; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (j (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] where the amount of tax is shown separately, a statement confirming that that tax may not be treated as the input tax of any person. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In paragraph (1)(j) above “input tax” has the same meaning as in section 24(1) of the Value Added Tax Act 1994 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00011]. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART IX (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — TEMPORARY DISPOSALS (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b3ca6440e41c9dd33a97eddd71e5a42][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 38 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Scope and effect (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b3ca6440e41c9dd33a97eddd71e5a42][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
