. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 40 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Disposals to be treated as made at certain times (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b3ca6440e41c9dd33a97eddd71e5a42][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART X (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### CHAPTER I (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — DETERMINATION OF WEIGHT OF MATERIAL DISPOSED OF (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) AT A LANDFILL SITE (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8bd6908c3fb212eb0a3ffefd520321a4][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-5191a7118e3a8bcef792ec538938f599][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

#### 41 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Scope (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31ae2a6b9dbb8948ba97e7ffafb83fff][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

This *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Chapter[^key-0397450b88992f6950b75fff82612b67] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] applies for the purpose of determining the weight of material comprised in a disposal *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] at a landfill site[^key-96e8b81828c071cb69409c60a6b02b92] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; and references in this Part to weight shall be construed as references to the weight of such material. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

#### 42 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Basic method (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-95776ce2ce40f8d4bfb93963eac2c2fa][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Except where regulation 43 or 44 applies and subject to paragraph (2) below, a registrable person shall determine weight by weighing the material concerned. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The weighing of the material shall be carried out at the time of the disposal; and for this purpose any time at which section 61 of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Act *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] requires[^key-863e4e06fffa1e041aa25932179d5b87] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the disposal to be treated as made shall be disregarded. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

#### 43 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Specified methods (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-7c30fc8bd52f883920cd588ec8e38cf4][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Except where regulation 44 applies, this regulation applies where the Commissioners have specified rules for determining weight in a notice published by them and not withdrawn by a further notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A specification made by the Commissioners as described in paragraph (1) above may make provision for— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the method by which weight is to be determined; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the time by reference to which weight is to be determined. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] A specification made by the Commissioners as described in paragraph (1) above may provide— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that it is to have effect only in relation to disposals of such descriptions as may be set out in the specification; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] that it is not to have effect in relation to particular disposals unless the Commissioners are satisfied that such conditions as may be set out in the specification are met in relation to the disposals. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where this regulation applies the registrable person shall determine weight in accordance with the rules in the specification (and not in accordance with the rule in regulation 42). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

#### 44 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Agreed methods (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-89b4f7045d7fa9f8586e64b3e3f58843][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] This regulation applies where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the registrable person and an authorised person have agreed in writing that weight shall be determined in accordance with rules other than those described in regulation 42 or specified under regulation 43; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a direction under paragraph (3) below has not been made. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Rules may be agreed under this regulation as regards— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the method by which weight is to be determined; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the time by reference to which weight is to be determined; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the discounting of water forming a constituent of material disposed of, but this is subject to paragraph (5) below. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where rules have been agreed under this regulation and the Commissioners believe that they should no longer be applied because they do not give an accurate indication of the weight or they are not being fully observed or for some other reason they may direct that the agreed rules shall no longer have effect. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where this regulation applies the registrable person shall determine weight in accordance with the rules agreed (and not in accordance with the rule in regulation 42 or 43). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraphs (6) to (8) below, rules may be agreed regarding the discounting of water if, and only if— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] no water is present in the material naturally and the water is present because— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it has been added for the purpose of enabling the material to be transported for disposal; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it has been used for the purpose of extracting any mineral; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it has arisen, or has been added, in the course of an industrial process; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the material is the residue from the treatment of effluent or sewage by a water treatment works. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Rules may not be agreed under paragraph (5) above where any of the material is capable of escaping from the landfill site concerned by leaching unless— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] it is likely to do so in the form of water only; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the leachate is to be collected on the site concerned and treated in order to eliminate any potential it has to cause harm. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the material falls within paragraph (5)(a) above rules may not be agreed under paragraph (5) above unless the total water which has been added, or (in a case falling within paragraph (5)(a)(iii) above) has arisen or has been added or both, constitutes 25 per cent. or more of the weight at the time of the disposal. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the material falls within paragraph (5)(b) above rules may not be agreed under paragraph (5) above except for the discounting of water which has been added prior to disposal (and not of water which is present in the material naturally). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of paragraph (8) above any water which has been extracted prior to disposal shall be deemed to be water that has been added, except that where the water extracted exceeds the quantity of water added that excess shall be deemed to have been present naturally. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### CHAPTER II: DETERMINATION OF WEIGHT OF MATERIAL DISPOSED OF AT A PLACE OTHER THAN A LANDFILL SITE (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-d276c266a80d97984e3cba725071f127][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

#### 44A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Scope (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-d31d0ceaba22e815e58887557a918e8c][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

This Chapter applies for the purposes of determining the weight of material comprised in a taxable disposal at a place other than a landfill site. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

#### 44B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Method (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The weight of material disposed of at a place other than a landfill site is to be determined in accordance with the prescribed rules set out in paragraph (2) and references in this Chapter to weight shall be construed as references to the weight of such material. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] The prescribed rules are that weight is to be determined— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] by reference to any records held by a taxable person relating to the volume of the material disposed of, provided that the Commissioners are reasonably satisfied as to the completeness and accuracy of those records; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] by using any reasonable method of estimation of the volume of the material disposed of, including, but not limited to, the use of technology; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] by applying a multiplier of 1.5 per cubic metre of material to its volume (whether determined under sub-paragraph (a)(i) or (ii)), in order to calculate its weight, expressed in tonnes (to one decimal place); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] without any discounting of constituents (including water); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] by reference to the material disposed of that is present at a place other than a landfill site at the time that the Commissioners carry out any inspection of that material, but they may also take into account— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] such other information that they may obtain in relation to material disposed of at that place that is no longer present at the time of any inspection (including from records held by a taxable person); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] any information provided to them by an environmental regulator; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] by the Commissioners making such reasonable apportionment as they can in the circumstances between the weight of the material and the weight of any other material previously disposed of and assessed to tax under section 50A of the Act or paragraph 31 of Schedule 12 to the Finance Act 2018. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

## PART XI (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) — SET-OFF OF AMOUNTS (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 45 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Landfill tax amount owed to Commissioners (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-d94644ca4a0b136664f9010e1750b32b][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to regulation 47, this regulation applies where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person is under a duty to pay to the Commissioners at any time an amount or amounts in respect of landfill tax; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Commissioners are under a duty to pay to that person at the same time an amount or amounts in respect of any tax or taxes under their care and management. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) above exceeds the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) above, the latter shall be set off against the former. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) above exceeds the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph(1)(a) above, the Commissioners may set off the latter in paying the former. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) above is the same as the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) above, no payment need be made in respect of either. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where this regulation applies and an amount has been set off in accordance with any of paragraphs (2) to (4) above, the duty of both the person and the Commissioners to pay the amount or amounts concerned shall be treated as having been discharged accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] References in paragraph (1) above to an amount in respect of a particular tax include references not only to an amount of tax itself but also to amounts of penalty, surcharge or interest. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] In this regulation “tax” includes “duty”. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 46 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Landfill tax amount owed by Commissioners (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-cd509db3833ecfefc55fdb7c70ba3405][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to regulation 47, this regulation applies where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] a person is under a duty to pay to the Commissioners at any time an amount or amounts in respect of any tax or taxes under their care and management; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the Commissioners are under a duty to pay to that person at the same time an amount or amounts in respect of landfill tax. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) above exceeds the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) above, the latter shall be set off against the former. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) above exceeds the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) above, the Commissioners may set off the latter in paying the former. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) above is the same as the total of the amount or amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) above, no payment need be made in respect of either. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Where this regulation applies and an amount has been set off in accordance with any of paragraphs (2) to (4) above, the duty of both the person and the Commissioners to pay the amount or amounts concerned shall be treated as having been discharged accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Paragraphs (6) and (7) of regulation 45 shall apply in relation to this regulation as they apply in relation to that regulation. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

### 47 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). No set-off where insolvency procedure applied (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-45d6b865a91dea59b2da18d43e3a293d][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Neither regulation 45 nor 46 shall require any such amount as is mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) of those regulations (in either case, “the credit”) to be set against any such sum as is mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) of those regulations (in either case, “the debit”) in any case where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] an insolvency procedure has been applied to the person entitled to the credit; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the credit became due after that procedure was so applied; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the liability to pay the debit either arose before that procedure was so applied or (having risen afterwards) relates to, or to matters occurring in the course of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the carrying on of any business; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] in the case of any sum such as is mentioned in regulation 46(1)(b), the carrying out of taxable activities, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

  at times before the procedure was so applied. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] Subject to paragraph (3) below, the following are the times when an insolvency procedure is to be taken, for the purposes of this regulation, to have been applied to any person, that is to say— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when a bankruptcy order, winding-up order, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-4047e239052e08dbe272bd17a48c9567]... or award of sequestration is made in relation to that person *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or that person enters administration[^key-5294207c76926d03e1803aeeee7d3b90] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when that person is put into administrative receivership; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when that person, being a corporation, passes a resolution for voluntary winding-up; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when any voluntary arrangement approved in accordance with Part I or Part VIII of the Insolvency Act 1986 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00012], or Part II or Chapter II of Part VIII of the Insolvency (Northern Ireland) Order 1989 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb][^f00013], comes into force in relation to that person; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when a deed of arrangement registered in accordance with *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-f794435c2cb2fea6f98da50a3a80d5ef]... Chapter I of Part VIII of that Order of 1989 takes effect in relation to that person; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (f (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when that person’s estate becomes vested in any other person as that person’s trustee under a trust deed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] References in this regulation, in relation to any person, to the application of an insolvency procedure to that person shall not include— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the making of a bankruptcy order, winding-up order, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] [^key-7bb64bd5a3fc6f8fd50c45bc64387fb7]... or award of sequestration *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] or that person entering administration[^key-f9a2c5442185f95cd4e20be1d7970e6a] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] at a time when any such arrangements or deed as is mentioned in paragraph (2)(d) to (f) above is in force in relation to that person; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the making of a winding-up order at any of the following times— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] immediately upon *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the appointment of the administrator ceasing to have effect[^key-d756de70f17a5138cf6bf5b74e1a1450] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when that person is being wound-up voluntarily; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
  - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] when that person is in administrative receivership; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] the making of an administration order in relation to that person at any time when that person is in administrative receivership. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

(4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb] For the purposes of this regulation a person shall be regarded as being in administrative receivership throughout any continuous period for which (disregarding any temporary vacancy in the office of receiver) there is an administrative receiver of that person, and the reference in paragraph (2) above to a person being put into administrative receivership shall be construed accordingly. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-3707a1a359805fa02292e81ab0b731fb]

[^key-27d0fb0bd47adf4fdc15b71c21224ddd]. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(This note is not part of the Regulations)

These Regulations make provision for the administration and assurance of landfill tax. In particular:

- Part I provides for the Regulations to come into force on 1st August 1996, defines expressions used in them and provides for certain decisions of the Commissioners to be made in writing.
- Part II regulates the registration of persons who intend to make taxable disposals. It provides for changes to the register and the removal from the register of persons who cease to intend to make taxable disposals. It also makes special provision for transfers of a going concern, partnerships and other unincorporated bodies and relating to the bankruptcy or incapacity of registered persons.
- Part III deals with accounting for tax by making returns, the keeping of a landfill tax account, the correction of errors, payment of the tax and retention of records.
- Part IV deals with claims and payments in respect of credits of tax arising under Parts V, VI and VII.
- Part V provides for an entitlement to credit in circumstances relating to the recycling, incineration and permanent removal of waste.
- Part VI provides for an entitlement to credit in respect of bad debts, and regulates the evidence required and records to be kept. It also provides for the attribution of payments to debts and the repayment of credit.
- Part VII provides for an entitlement to credit for contributions made to bodies for expenditure on approved environmental objects. It also defines the roles and responsibilities of a special regulatory body and Customs and Excise.
- Part VIII specifies what information a landfill invoice must contain.
- Part IX deals with temporary disposals, and makes special provision for the disposal to be exempt or for the tax to be deferred.
- Part X sets out the basic method of determining the weight of material disposed of, and provides for specified and agreed methods to be used instead.
- Part XI provides for the set-off of landfill tax credits and debits against credits and debits arising from other taxes and duties administered by Customs and Excise, and prevents the set-off of credits in cases of insolvency.
- Part XII provides for distress to be levied in respect of landfill tax debts and for certain things relating to diligence in Scotland to be done by officers of Customs and Excise of a minimum grade.
